./2DTextureArray.cxx:341:        blkTextureArrayImageView = VK_NULL_HANDLE;

./Chunk.cxx:1:// Chunk.cpp — optimized
./Chunk.cxx:2://
./Chunk.cxx:3:// Key changes:
./Chunk.cxx:4://   1. makeVisible: cache neighbor chunks once per edge; avoid per-voxel gop
./Chunk.cxx:5://   calls
./Chunk.cxx:6://   2. generateMesh: single pass to bucket visible voxels by type; greedy only
./Chunk.cxx:7://   iterates its own bucket
./Chunk.cxx:8://   3. Mask arrays allocated once outside loops, cleared with memset
./Chunk.cxx:9://   4. Per-layer hasVisible flag skips fully-buried layers entirely
./Chunk.cxx:10://   5. vertices/indices reserved upfront
./Chunk.cxx:11://   6. emmitQuad: pre-built UV array, avoid repeated glm::vec2 construction
./Chunk.cxx:12:
./Chunk.cxx:13:#include "Chunk.h"
./Chunk.cxx:14:#include "GameObjectPool.h"
./Chunk.cxx:15:#include "GraphicsPipeline.h"
./Chunk.cxx:16:#include "Terrain.h"
./Chunk.cxx:17:#include <cstdint>
./Chunk.cxx:18:#include <fmt/base.h>
./Chunk.cxx:19:#include <fmt/core.h>
./Chunk.cxx:20:
./Chunk.cxx:21:#include "PerlinNoise.hpp"
./Chunk.cxx:22:
./Chunk.cxx:23:#include <array>
./Chunk.cxx:24:#include <cstring> // memset
./Chunk.cxx:25:#include <iostream>
./Chunk.cxx:26:
./Chunk.cxx:27:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
./Chunk.cxx:28:// Construction — unchanged logic, just tidied
./Chunk.cxx:29:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
./Chunk.cxx:30:Chunk::Chunk(int cmx, int cmy, VulkanContext &vkContext, GameObjectPool &gop)
./Chunk.cxx:31:    : vkContext{vkContext}, chunkMesh{vkContext}, gop{gop} {
./Chunk.cxx:32:  chunkOffset[0] = cmx;
./Chunk.cxx:33:  chunkOffset[1] = cmy;
./Chunk.cxx:34:
./Chunk.cxx:35:  const float baseX = cmx * 16;
./Chunk.cxx:36:  const float baseY = cmy * 16;
./Chunk.cxx:37:
./Chunk.cxx:38:  float freq = .05f;
./Chunk.cxx:39:  int octv = 1;
./Chunk.cxx:40:  uint32_t seed = 32846347;
./Chunk.cxx:41:  int baseZ = 60;
./Chunk.cxx:42:
./Chunk.cxx:43:  const siv::PerlinNoise perlin{seed};
./Chunk.cxx:44:
./Chunk.cxx:45:  for (int z = 0; z < 256; ++z) {
./Chunk.cxx:46:    auto &layer = layers[z];
./Chunk.cxx:47:    layer.z = z;
./Chunk.cxx:48:    layer.hasVisible = false; // initialise the new flag
./Chunk.cxx:49:
./Chunk.cxx:50:    for (int x = 0; x < 16; ++x) {
./Chunk.cxx:51:      const float px = baseX + x;
./Chunk.cxx:52:      for (int y = 0; y < 16; ++y) {
./Chunk.cxx:53:        Voxel &v = layer.voxels[x][y];
./Chunk.cxx:54:
./Chunk.cxx:55:        int grassZ = baseZ + 2 * perlin.octave2D_11(
./Chunk.cxx:56:                                     (x + 16 * chunkOffset[0]) * freq,
./Chunk.cxx:57:                                     (y + 16 * chunkOffset[1]) * freq, octv);
./Chunk.cxx:58:        if (z < grassZ)
./Chunk.cxx:59:          v.setType(GRASS);
./Chunk.cxx:60:
./Chunk.cxx:61:        if (z == 0)
./Chunk.cxx:62:          v.setType(BEDROCK);
./Chunk.cxx:63:      }
./Chunk.cxx:64:    }
./Chunk.cxx:65:  }
./Chunk.cxx:66:}
./Chunk.cxx:67:
./Chunk.cxx:68:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
./Chunk.cxx:69:// makeVisible — OPTIMISED
./Chunk.cxx:70://   * Resolve the four edge-neighboring chunks ONCE per call instead of
./Chunk.cxx:71://     calling gop.getVoxelGlobal up to 4× per voxel.
./Chunk.cxx:72://   * Store raw Chunk* pointers for the four cardinal neighbors so inner-loop
./Chunk.cxx:73://     neighbor lookups are a single array index rather than a hash/map lookup.
./Chunk.cxx:74://   * Set layer.hasVisible so generateMesh can skip dead layers cheaply.
./Chunk.cxx:75:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
./Chunk.cxx:76:void Chunk::makeVisible() {
./Chunk.cxx:77:  // Fetch the four cardinal neighbor chunks once.
./Chunk.cxx:78:  // terrain->getChunkByKey() is assumed to return nullTerrain::chunkKeyptr for
./Chunk.cxx:79:  // unloaded) chunks.
./Chunk.cxx:80:  const int cx = chunkOffset[0];
./Chunk.cxx:81:  const int cy = chunkOffset[1];
./Chunk.cxx:82:
./Chunk.cxx:83:  Chunk *chunkLeft =
./Chunk.cxx:84:      gop.terrain->getChunkByKey(Terrain::chunkKey(cx, cy - 1)); // y-1
./Chunk.cxx:85:  Chunk *chunkRight =
./Chunk.cxx:86:      gop.terrain->getChunkByKey(Terrain::chunkKey(cx, cy + 1)); // y+1
./Chunk.cxx:87:  Chunk *chunkFront =
./Chunk.cxx:88:      gop.terrain->getChunkByKey(Terrain::chunkKey(cx + 1, cy)); // x+1
./Chunk.cxx:89:  Chunk *chunkBack =
./Chunk.cxx:90:      gop.terrain->getChunkByKey(Terrain::chunkKey(cx - 1, cy)); // x-1
./Chunk.cxx:91:
./Chunk.cxx:92:  for (int z = 0; z < 256; ++z) {
./Chunk.cxx:93:    Layer &layer = layers[z];
./Chunk.cxx:94:    layer.hasVisible = false;
./Chunk.cxx:95:
./Chunk.cxx:96:    for (int x = 0; x < 16; ++x) {
./Chunk.cxx:97:      for (int y = 0; y < 16; ++y) {
./Chunk.cxx:98:        Voxel &v = layer.voxels[x][y];
./Chunk.cxx:99:
./Chunk.cxx:100:        if (v.blockType == AIR) {
./Chunk.cxx:101:          v.visible = false;
./Chunk.cxx:102:          continue;
./Chunk.cxx:103:        }
./Chunk.cxx:104:
./Chunk.cxx:105:        if (z == 0 || z == 255) [[unlikely]] {
./Chunk.cxx:106:          v.visible = true;
./Chunk.cxx:107:          layer.hasVisible = true;
./Chunk.cxx:108:          continue;
./Chunk.cxx:109:        }
./Chunk.cxx:110:
./Chunk.cxx:111:        // Vertical neighbors are always within this chunk.
./Chunk.cxx:112:        if (layers[z + 1].voxels[x][y].blockType == AIR ||
./Chunk.cxx:113:            layers[z - 1].voxels[x][y].blockType == AIR) {
./Chunk.cxx:114:          v.visible = true;
./Chunk.cxx:115:          layer.hasVisible = true;
./Chunk.cxx:116:          continue;
./Chunk.cxx:117:        }
./Chunk.cxx:118:
./Chunk.cxx:119:        // Lateral neighbors — use chunk pointers for edge voxels.
./Chunk.cxx:120:        bool nextToAir = false;
./Chunk.cxx:121:
./Chunk.cxx:122:        // left (y-1)
./Chunk.cxx:123:        if (y > 0) {
./Chunk.cxx:124:          nextToAir |= layer.voxels[x][y - 1].blockType == AIR;
./Chunk.cxx:125:        } else {
./Chunk.cxx:126:          nextToAir |=
./Chunk.cxx:127:              !chunkLeft || chunkLeft->layers[z].voxels[x][15].blockType == AIR;
./Chunk.cxx:128:        }
./Chunk.cxx:129:
./Chunk.cxx:130:        // right (y+1)
./Chunk.cxx:131:        if (!nextToAir) {
./Chunk.cxx:132:          if (y < 15) {
./Chunk.cxx:133:            nextToAir |= layer.voxels[x][y + 1].blockType == AIR;
./Chunk.cxx:134:          } else {
./Chunk.cxx:135:            nextToAir |= !chunkRight ||
./Chunk.cxx:136:                         chunkRight->layers[z].voxels[x][0].blockType == AIR;
./Chunk.cxx:137:          }
./Chunk.cxx:138:        }
./Chunk.cxx:139:
./Chunk.cxx:140:        // front (x+1)
./Chunk.cxx:141:        if (!nextToAir) {
./Chunk.cxx:142:          if (x < 15) {
./Chunk.cxx:143:            nextToAir |= layer.voxels[x + 1][y].blockType == AIR;
./Chunk.cxx:144:          } else {
./Chunk.cxx:145:            nextToAir |= !chunkFront ||
./Chunk.cxx:146:                         chunkFront->layers[z].voxels[0][y].blockType == AIR;
./Chunk.cxx:147:          }
./Chunk.cxx:148:        }
./Chunk.cxx:149:
./Chunk.cxx:150:        // back (x-1)
./Chunk.cxx:151:        if (!nextToAir) {
./Chunk.cxx:152:          if (x > 0) {
./Chunk.cxx:153:            nextToAir |= layer.voxels[x - 1][y].blockType == AIR;
./Chunk.cxx:154:          } else {
./Chunk.cxx:155:            nextToAir |= !chunkBack ||
./Chunk.cxx:156:                         chunkBack->layers[z].voxels[15][y].blockType == AIR;
./Chunk.cxx:157:          }
./Chunk.cxx:158:        }
./Chunk.cxx:159:
./Chunk.cxx:160:        v.visible = nextToAir;
./Chunk.cxx:161:        if (nextToAir)
./Chunk.cxx:162:          layer.hasVisible = true;
./Chunk.cxx:163:      }
./Chunk.cxx:164:    }
./Chunk.cxx:165:  }
./Chunk.cxx:166:}
./Chunk.cxx:167:
./Chunk.cxx:168:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
./Chunk.cxx:169:// generateMesh — OPTIMISED
./Chunk.cxx:170://   * Reserve vertex/index storage upfront (rough upper bound).
./Chunk.cxx:171://   * Single pre-pass: bucket visible voxel coordinates by BlockType.
./Chunk.cxx:172://   * Greedy lambdas consume their own buckets instead of re-scanning all 65k
./Chunk.cxx:173://     voxels per block type.
./Chunk.cxx:174://   * Mask arrays allocated ONCE outside loops; cleared with memset.
./Chunk.cxx:175://   * emmitQuad uses a precomputed UV table.
./Chunk.cxx:176:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
./Chunk.cxx:177:void Chunk::generateMesh() {
./Chunk.cxx:178:  TIMER_START(mesh);
./Chunk.cxx:179:
./Chunk.cxx:180:  std::vector<Vertex> vertices;
./Chunk.cxx:181:  std::vector<uint32_t> indices;
./Chunk.cxx:182:
./Chunk.cxx:183:  // Reserve a generous upper bound. A worst-case fully exposed chunk has
./Chunk.cxx:184:  // 16*16*256 = 65 536 voxels × 6 faces × 4 verts = ~1.57M verts — but
./Chunk.cxx:185:  // greedy meshing collapses most of them. 128k is a practical ceiling.
./Chunk.cxx:186:  vertices.reserve(128 * 1024);
./Chunk.cxx:187:  indices.reserve(192 * 1024);
./Chunk.cxx:188:
./Chunk.cxx:189:  int ind = 0;
./Chunk.cxx:190:
./Chunk.cxx:191:  const float ox = chunkOffset[0] * 16.0f;
./Chunk.cxx:192:  const float oy = chunkOffset[1] * 16.0f;
./Chunk.cxx:193:
./Chunk.cxx:194:  // Pre-built UV table: uv[i] = glm::vec2{(int)type, i}  — avoids
./Chunk.cxx:195:  // rebuilding the same 4 vec2s for every quad.
./Chunk.cxx:196:  // We pass the texture index in and index into this at emit time.
./Chunk.cxx:197:  auto emmitQuad = [&](glm::vec3 p0, glm::vec3 p1, glm::vec3 p2, glm::vec3 p3,
./Chunk.cxx:198:                       int tex, float brightness) {
./Chunk.cxx:199:    const float width = glm::length(p3 - p0);
./Chunk.cxx:200:    const float height = glm::length(p1 - p0);
./Chunk.cxx:201:    const glm::vec3 size{width, height, 0};
./Chunk.cxx:202:
./Chunk.cxx:203:    const glm::vec2 uv0{tex, 0}, uv1{tex, 1}, uv2{tex, 2}, uv3{tex, 3};
./Chunk.cxx:204:    vertices.push_back({p0, size, uv0, brightness});
./Chunk.cxx:205:    vertices.push_back({p1, size, uv1, brightness});
./Chunk.cxx:206:    vertices.push_back({p2, size, uv2, brightness});
./Chunk.cxx:207:    vertices.push_back({p3, size, uv3, brightness});
./Chunk.cxx:208:    const uint32_t base = ind * 4;
./Chunk.cxx:209:    indices.push_back(base);
./Chunk.cxx:210:    indices.push_back(base + 1);
./Chunk.cxx:211:    indices.push_back(base + 2);
./Chunk.cxx:212:    indices.push_back(base + 2);
./Chunk.cxx:213:    indices.push_back(base + 3);
./Chunk.cxx:214:    indices.push_back(base);
./Chunk.cxx:215:    ++ind;
./Chunk.cxx:216:  };
./Chunk.cxx:217:
./Chunk.cxx:218:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------
./Chunk.cxx:219:  // TOP / BOTTOM faces — one pass per layer, skips invisible layers.
./Chunk.cxx:220:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------
./Chunk.cxx:221:  // Masks allocated once, reset per layer with memset.
./Chunk.cxx:222:  bool topMask[16][16], botMask[16][16];
./Chunk.cxx:223:  bool topUsed[16][16], botUsed[16][16];
./Chunk.cxx:224:
./Chunk.cxx:225:  for (int z = 0; z < 256; ++z) {
./Chunk.cxx:226:    Layer &layer = layers[z];
./Chunk.cxx:227:    if (!layer.hasVisible)
./Chunk.cxx:228:      continue; // entire layer buried — skip
./Chunk.cxx:229:
./Chunk.cxx:230:    memset(topMask, 0, sizeof(topMask));
./Chunk.cxx:231:    memset(botMask, 0, sizeof(botMask));
./Chunk.cxx:232:
./Chunk.cxx:233:    for (int x = 0; x < 16; ++x) {
./Chunk.cxx:234:      for (int y = 0; y < 16; ++y) {
./Chunk.cxx:235:        Voxel &v = layer.voxels[x][y];
./Chunk.cxx:236:        if (!v.visible)
./Chunk.cxx:237:          continue;
./Chunk.cxx:238:        topMask[x][y] =
./Chunk.cxx:239:            (z == 255) || (layers[z + 1].voxels[x][y].blockType == AIR);
./Chunk.cxx:240:        botMask[x][y] =
./Chunk.cxx:241:            (z == 0) || (layers[z - 1].voxels[x][y].blockType == AIR);
./Chunk.cxx:242:      }
./Chunk.cxx:243:    }
./Chunk.cxx:244:
./Chunk.cxx:245:    // --- TOP ---
./Chunk.cxx:246:    memset(topUsed, 0, sizeof(topUsed));
./Chunk.cxx:247:    for (int x = 0; x < 16; ++x) {
./Chunk.cxx:248:      for (int y = 0; y < 16; ++y) {
./Chunk.cxx:249:        if (!topMask[x][y] || topUsed[x][y])
./Chunk.cxx:250:          continue;
./Chunk.cxx:251:        const int tex = layer.voxels[x][y].faceTexture[TOP];
./Chunk.cxx:252:        int h = 1;
./Chunk.cxx:253:        while (y + h < 16 && topMask[x][y + h] && !topUsed[x][y + h] &&
./Chunk.cxx:254:               layer.voxels[x][y + h].faceTexture[TOP] == tex)
./Chunk.cxx:255:          ++h;
./Chunk.cxx:256:        int w = 1;
./Chunk.cxx:257:        while (x + w < 16) {
./Chunk.cxx:258:          bool ok = true;
./Chunk.cxx:259:          for (int dy = 0; dy < h && ok; ++dy)
./Chunk.cxx:260:            if (!topMask[x + w][y + dy] || topUsed[x + w][y + dy] ||
./Chunk.cxx:261:                layer.voxels[x + w][y + dy].faceTexture[TOP] != tex)
./Chunk.cxx:262:              ok = false;
./Chunk.cxx:263:          if (!ok)
./Chunk.cxx:264:            break;
./Chunk.cxx:265:          ++w;
./Chunk.cxx:266:        }
./Chunk.cxx:267:        for (int dx = 0; dx < w; ++dx)
./Chunk.cxx:268:          for (int dy = 0; dy < h; ++dy)
./Chunk.cxx:269:            topUsed[x + dx][y + dy] = true;
./Chunk.cxx:270:
./Chunk.cxx:271:        emmitQuad({ox + x, oy + y, (float)z + 1},
./Chunk.cxx:272:                  {ox + x + w, oy + y, (float)z + 1},
./Chunk.cxx:273:                  {ox + x + w, oy + y + h, (float)z + 1},
./Chunk.cxx:274:                  {ox + x, oy + y + h, (float)z + 1}, tex, 1.0f);
./Chunk.cxx:275:      }
./Chunk.cxx:276:    }
./Chunk.cxx:277:
./Chunk.cxx:278:    // --- BOTTOM ---

./PerlinNoise.hpp:249:		////////////////////////////////////////////////
./PerlinNoise.hpp:250:		//
./PerlinNoise.hpp:251:		//	These functions are provided for consistency.
./PerlinNoise.hpp:252:		//	You may get different results from std::shuffle() with different standard library implementations.
./PerlinNoise.hpp:253:		//
./PerlinNoise.hpp:254:		SIVPERLIN_CONCEPT_URBG
./PerlinNoise.hpp:255:		[[nodiscard]]
./PerlinNoise.hpp:256:		inline std::uint64_t Random(const std::uint64_t max, URBG&& urbg)
./PerlinNoise.hpp:257:		{
./PerlinNoise.hpp:258:			return (urbg() % (max + 1));
./PerlinNoise.hpp:259:		}
./PerlinNoise.hpp:260:
./PerlinNoise.hpp:261:		template <class RandomIt, class URBG>
./PerlinNoise.hpp:262:		inline void Shuffle(RandomIt first, RandomIt last, URBG&& urbg)
./PerlinNoise.hpp:263:		{
./PerlinNoise.hpp:264:			if (first == last)
./PerlinNoise.hpp:265:			{
./PerlinNoise.hpp:266:				return;
./PerlinNoise.hpp:267:			}
./PerlinNoise.hpp:268:
./PerlinNoise.hpp:269:			using difference_type = typename std::iterator_traits<RandomIt>::difference_type;
./PerlinNoise.hpp:270:
./PerlinNoise.hpp:271:			for (RandomIt it = first + 1; it < last; ++it)
./PerlinNoise.hpp:272:			{
./PerlinNoise.hpp:273:				const std::uint64_t n = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(it - first);
./PerlinNoise.hpp:274:				std::iter_swap(it, first + static_cast<difference_type>(Random(n, std::forward<URBG>(urbg))));
./PerlinNoise.hpp:275:			}
./PerlinNoise.hpp:276:		}
./PerlinNoise.hpp:277:		//
./PerlinNoise.hpp:278:		////////////////////////////////////////////////
./PerlinNoise.hpp:279:
./PerlinNoise.hpp:280:		template <class Float>
./PerlinNoise.hpp:281:		[[nodiscard]]
./PerlinNoise.hpp:282:		inline constexpr Float Fade(const Float t) noexcept
./PerlinNoise.hpp:283:		{
./PerlinNoise.hpp:284:			return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10);
./PerlinNoise.hpp:285:		}
./PerlinNoise.hpp:286:
./PerlinNoise.hpp:287:		template <class Float>
./PerlinNoise.hpp:288:		[[nodiscard]]

./Player.cxx:26:void Player::handlePlayerMovement(UniformBufferObject &UBO,
./Player.cxx:27:                                  VkExtent2D &swapChainExtent, Event &event) {
./Player.cxx:28:  static float deltaJZ = 0;
./Player.cxx:29:
./Player.cxx:30:  double dx = 0, dy = 0, dz = 0;
./Player.cxx:31:
./Player.cxx:32:  selfTransform.position = camera->cameraPos;
./Player.cxx:33:  selfTransform.position.z += .2f;
./Player.cxx:34:
./Player.cxx:35:  aabb.max = selfTransform.position + glm::vec3(.25f, .25f, .0f);
./Player.cxx:36:  aabb.min = selfTransform.position - glm::vec3(.25f, .25f, 1.8f);
./Player.cxx:37:
./Player.cxx:38:  AxisAlignedBoundingBox groundAABB{};
./Player.cxx:39:  groundAABB.max = selfTransform.position + glm::vec3(.1f, .1f, .0f);
./Player.cxx:40:  groundAABB.min = selfTransform.position - glm::vec3(.1f, .1f, 1.8f);
./Player.cxx:41:
./Player.cxx:42:  // ── ground check ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
./Player.cxx:43:  playerState.onGround = false;
./Player.cxx:44:  {
./Player.cxx:45:    int feet = floor(groundAABB.min.z);
./Player.cxx:46:    int head = floor(groundAABB.max.z);
./Player.cxx:47:    int minX = floor(groundAABB.min.x);
./Player.cxx:48:    int maxX = floor(groundAABB.max.x);
./Player.cxx:49:    int minY = floor(groundAABB.min.y);
./Player.cxx:50:    int maxY = floor(groundAABB.max.y);
./Player.cxx:51:
./Player.cxx:52:    for (int k = feet; k <= head && !playerState.onGround; k++)
./Player.cxx:53:      for (int i = minX; i <= maxX && !playerState.onGround; i++)
./Player.cxx:54:        for (int j = minY; j <= maxY; j++) {
./Player.cxx:55:          auto v = gameObjectPool.getVoxelGlobal({i, j, k});
./Player.cxx:56:          if (v && v->blockType != AIR) {
./Player.cxx:57:            playerState.onGround = true;
./Player.cxx:58:            break;
./Player.cxx:59:          }
./Player.cxx:60:        }
./Player.cxx:61:  }
./Player.cxx:62:
./Player.cxx:63:  // ── gravity / jump ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
./Player.cxx:64:  if (!playerState.onGround && !playerState.inJump)
./Player.cxx:65:    dz -= .01f * event.dt;
./Player.cxx:66:  else if (playerState.inJump) {
./Player.cxx:67:    deltaJZ -= .003f * event.dt;
./Player.cxx:68:    dz += .01f * event.dt * deltaJZ;
./Player.cxx:69:  }
./Player.cxx:70:
./Player.cxx:71:  // ── desired XY movement ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
./Player.cxx:72:  float x_fac = cos(glm::radians(camera->yaw));
./Player.cxx:73:  float y_fac = sin(glm::radians(camera->yaw));
./Player.cxx:74:  float x_fac_dx = x_fac * camera->speed * event.dt;
./Player.cxx:75:  float y_fac_dy = y_fac * camera->speed * event.dt;
./Player.cxx:76:
./Player.cxx:77:  // Update facing direction for animation / other systems — NOT used for
./Player.cxx:78:  // collision
./Player.cxx:79:  bool xDominant = std::abs(x_fac) > std::abs(y_fac);
./Player.cxx:80:  if (x_fac > 0 && xDominant)
./Player.cxx:81:    facingDirection = NORTH;
./Player.cxx:82:  else if (x_fac < 0 && xDominant)
./Player.cxx:83:    facingDirection = SOUTH;
./Player.cxx:84:  else if (y_fac > 0 && !xDominant)
./Player.cxx:85:    facingDirection = WEST;
./Player.cxx:86:  else if (y_fac < 0 && !xDominant)
./Player.cxx:87:    facingDirection = EAST;
./Player.cxx:88:
./Player.cxx:89:  bool KEY_W = event.getKeyPressed(GLFW_KEY_W);
./Player.cxx:90:  bool KEY_S = event.getKeyPressed(GLFW_KEY_S);
./Player.cxx:91:  bool KEY_A = event.getKeyPressed(GLFW_KEY_A);
./Player.cxx:92:  bool KEY_D = event.getKeyPressed(GLFW_KEY_D);
./Player.cxx:93:
./Player.cxx:94:  if (KEY_W) {
./Player.cxx:95:    dx += x_fac_dx;
./Player.cxx:96:    dy += y_fac_dy;
./Player.cxx:97:  }
./Player.cxx:98:  if (KEY_S) {
./Player.cxx:99:    dx -= x_fac_dx;
./Player.cxx:100:    dy -= y_fac_dy;
./Player.cxx:101:  }
./Player.cxx:102:  if (KEY_A) {
./Player.cxx:103:    dx += -y_fac_dy;
./Player.cxx:104:    dy += x_fac_dx;
./Player.cxx:105:  }
./Player.cxx:106:  if (KEY_D) {
./Player.cxx:107:    dx -= -y_fac_dy;
./Player.cxx:108:    dy -= x_fac_dx;
./Player.cxx:109:  }
./Player.cxx:110:
./Player.cxx:111:  // ── axis-separated collision ─────────────────────────────────────────────
./Player.cxx:112:  // Helper: does the AABB at `testPos` overlap any solid voxel?
./Player.cxx:113:  auto overlaps = [&](glm::vec3 testPos) -> bool {
./Player.cxx:114:    glm::vec3 tMax = testPos + glm::vec3(.25f, .25f, .0f);
./Player.cxx:115:    glm::vec3 tMin = testPos - glm::vec3(.25f, .25f, 1.8f);
./Player.cxx:116:
./Player.cxx:117:    int x0 = floor(tMin.x), x1 = floor(tMax.x);
./Player.cxx:118:    int y0 = floor(tMin.y), y1 = floor(tMax.y);
./Player.cxx:119:    int z0 = floor(tMin.z) + 1, z1 = floor(tMax.z);
./Player.cxx:120:
./Player.cxx:121:    for (int k = z0; k <= z1; k++)
./Player.cxx:122:      for (int i = x0; i <= x1; i++)
./Player.cxx:123:        for (int j = y0; j <= y1; j++) {
./Player.cxx:124:          auto v = gameObjectPool.getVoxelGlobal({i, j, k});
./Player.cxx:125:          if (v && v->blockType != AIR)
./Player.cxx:126:            return true;
./Player.cxx:127:        }
./Player.cxx:128:    return false;
./Player.cxx:129:  };
./Player.cxx:130:
./Player.cxx:131:  glm::vec3 base = selfTransform.position;
./Player.cxx:132:
./Player.cxx:133:  if (overlaps(base + glm::vec3(dx, 0, 0)))
./Player.cxx:134:    dx = 0;
./Player.cxx:135:
./Player.cxx:136:  if (overlaps(base + glm::vec3(0, dy, 0)))
./Player.cxx:137:    dy = 0;
./Player.cxx:138:
./Player.cxx:139:  if (overlaps(base + glm::vec3(0, 0, dz)))
./Player.cxx:140:    dz = 0;
./Player.cxx:141:
./Player.cxx:142:  if (playerState.onGround) [[likely]]
./Player.cxx:143:    playerState.inJump = false;
./Player.cxx:144:
./Player.cxx:145:  if (event.getKeyPressed(GLFW_KEY_SPACE) && playerState.onGround) {
./Player.cxx:146:    deltaJZ = 1.f;
./Player.cxx:147:    playerState.inJump = true;
./Player.cxx:148:  }
./Player.cxx:149:
./Player.cxx:150:  camera->cameraPos.x += dx;
./Player.cxx:151:  camera->cameraPos.y += dy;
./Player.cxx:152:  camera->cameraPos.z += dz;
./Player.cxx:153:
./Player.cxx:154:  camera->locked = playerState.inInventory;
./Player.cxx:155:
./Player.cxx:156:  camera->updateUBO(UBO, swapChainExtent, event);
./Player.cxx:157:}

./Terrain.cxx:121:
./Terrain.cxx:122:void Terrain::sweepHandleTerrain(int playerChunkX, int playerChunkY) {
./Terrain.cxx:123:  std::lock_guard<std::recursive_mutex> lock(chunkMutex);
./Terrain.cxx:124:
./Terrain.cxx:125:  std::vector<uint64_t> toRemove;
./Terrain.cxx:126:  for (auto &[key, chunk] : chunks) {
./Terrain.cxx:127:    int cx = (int)(key >> 32);
./Terrain.cxx:128:    int cy = (int)(key & 0xFFFFFFFF);
./Terrain.cxx:129:    if (std::abs(cx - playerChunkX) > RENDER_DISTANCE ||
./Terrain.cxx:130:        std::abs(cy - playerChunkY) > RENDER_DISTANCE) {
./Terrain.cxx:131:      toRemove.push_back(key);
./Terrain.cxx:132:    }
./Terrain.cxx:133:  }
./Terrain.cxx:134:
./Terrain.cxx:135:  for (uint64_t key : toRemove) {
./Terrain.cxx:136:    chunks[key]->cleanup();
./Terrain.cxx:137:    delete chunks[key];
./Terrain.cxx:138:    chunks.erase(key);
./Terrain.cxx:139:    nChunksLoaded--;
./Terrain.cxx:140:  }
./Terrain.cxx:141:}

./Terrain.cxx:146:  chunkMutex.unlock();
./Terrain.cxx:147:}

./Textures.cxx:163:void u_Texture::createTextureImage() {

./Textures.cxx:182:               VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_VISIBLE_BIT |

./Textures.cxx:185:

./Textures.cxx:246:// before copying the image to the buffer we need to set it into the right
./Textures.cxx:247:// layout
./Textures.cxx:248:void u_Texture::transitionImageLayout(VkImage image, VkFormat format,
./Textures.cxx:249:                                      VkImageLayout oldLayout,
./Textures.cxx:250:                                      VkImageLayout newLayout,
./Textures.cxx:251:                                      uint32_t mipLevels) {
./Textures.cxx:252:  VkCommandBuffer commandBuffer = beginSingleTimeCommands();
./Textures.cxx:253:
./Textures.cxx:254:  VkImageMemoryBarrier barrier{};
./Textures.cxx:255:  barrier.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_MEMORY_BARRIER;
./Textures.cxx:256:  barrier.oldLayout = oldLayout;
./Textures.cxx:257:  barrier.newLayout = newLayout;
./Textures.cxx:258:
./Textures.cxx:259:  // We are not transfering queue family ownership rn!!!
./Textures.cxx:260:  barrier.srcQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
./Textures.cxx:261:  barrier.dstQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
./Textures.cxx:262:
./Textures.cxx:263:  barrier.image = image;
./Textures.cxx:264:  barrier.subresourceRange.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
./Textures.cxx:265:  barrier.subresourceRange.baseMipLevel = 0;
./Textures.cxx:266:  barrier.subresourceRange.levelCount = mipLevels;

./Textures.cxx:268:  barrier.subresourceRange.layerCount = 1;

./Textures.cxx:322:
./Textures.cxx:323:void u_Texture::generateMipmaps(VkImage image, VkFormat imageFormat,
./Textures.cxx:324:                                int32_t texWidth, int32_t texHeight,
./Textures.cxx:325:                                uint32_t mipLevels) {
./Textures.cxx:326:  // Check if image format supports linear blitting
./Textures.cxx:327:  VkFormatProperties formatProperties;
./Textures.cxx:328:  vkGetPhysicalDeviceFormatProperties(physicalDevice, imageFormat,
./Textures.cxx:329:                                      &formatProperties);
./Textures.cxx:330:
./Textures.cxx:331:  if (!(formatProperties.optimalTilingFeatures &
./Textures.cxx:332:        VK_FORMAT_FEATURE_SAMPLED_IMAGE_FILTER_LINEAR_BIT)) {
./Textures.cxx:333:    throw std::runtime_error(
./Textures.cxx:334:        "texture image format does not support linear blitting!");
./Textures.cxx:335:  }
./Textures.cxx:336:
./Textures.cxx:337:  VkCommandBuffer commandBuffer = beginSingleTimeCommands();
./Textures.cxx:338:
./Textures.cxx:339:  VkImageMemoryBarrier barrier{};
./Textures.cxx:340:  barrier.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_MEMORY_BARRIER;
./Textures.cxx:341:  barrier.image = image;
./Textures.cxx:342:  barrier.srcQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
./Textures.cxx:343:  barrier.dstQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
./Textures.cxx:344:  barrier.subresourceRange.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
./Textures.cxx:345:  barrier.subresourceRange.baseArrayLayer = 0;
./Textures.cxx:346:  barrier.subresourceRange.layerCount = 1;
./Textures.cxx:347:  barrier.subresourceRange.levelCount = 1;
./Textures.cxx:348:
./Textures.cxx:349:  int32_t mipWidth = texWidth;
./Textures.cxx:350:  int32_t mipHeight = texHeight;
./Textures.cxx:351:
./Textures.cxx:352:  for (uint32_t i = 1; i < mipLevels; i++) {
./Textures.cxx:353:    barrier.subresourceRange.baseMipLevel = i - 1;
./Textures.cxx:354:    barrier.oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL;
./Textures.cxx:355:    barrier.newLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL;
./Textures.cxx:356:    barrier.srcAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT;
./Textures.cxx:357:    barrier.dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_READ_BIT;
./Textures.cxx:358:
./Textures.cxx:359:    vkCmdPipelineBarrier(commandBuffer, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT,
./Textures.cxx:360:                         VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT, 0, 0, nullptr, 0,
./Textures.cxx:361:                         nullptr, 1, &barrier);
./Textures.cxx:362:
./Textures.cxx:363:    VkImageBlit blit{};
./Textures.cxx:364:    blit.srcOffsets[0] = {0, 0, 0};
./Textures.cxx:365:    blit.srcOffsets[1] = {mipWidth, mipHeight, 1};
./Textures.cxx:366:    blit.srcSubresource.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
./Textures.cxx:367:    blit.srcSubresource.mipLevel = i - 1;
./Textures.cxx:368:    blit.srcSubresource.baseArrayLayer = 0;
./Textures.cxx:369:    blit.srcSubresource.layerCount = 1;
./Textures.cxx:370:    blit.dstOffsets[0] = {0, 0, 0};
./Textures.cxx:371:    blit.dstOffsets[1] = {mipWidth > 1 ? mipWidth / 2 : 1,
./Textures.cxx:372:                          mipHeight > 1 ? mipHeight / 2 : 1, 1};
./Textures.cxx:373:    blit.dstSubresource.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
./Textures.cxx:374:    blit.dstSubresource.mipLevel = i;
./Textures.cxx:375:    blit.dstSubresource.baseArrayLayer = 0;
./Textures.cxx:376:    blit.dstSubresource.layerCount = 1;
./Textures.cxx:377:
./Textures.cxx:378:    vkCmdBlitImage(commandBuffer, image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL,
./Textures.cxx:379:                   image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL, 1, &blit,
./Textures.cxx:380:                   VK_FILTER_LINEAR);
./Textures.cxx:381:
./Textures.cxx:382:    barrier.oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL;
./Textures.cxx:383:    barrier.newLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL;
./Textures.cxx:384:    barrier.srcAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_READ_BIT;
./Textures.cxx:385:    barrier.dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_SHADER_READ_BIT;
./Textures.cxx:386:
./Textures.cxx:387:    vkCmdPipelineBarrier(commandBuffer, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT,
./Textures.cxx:388:                         VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_FRAGMENT_SHADER_BIT, 0, 0, nullptr,
./Textures.cxx:389:                         0, nullptr, 1, &barrier);
./Textures.cxx:390:
./Textures.cxx:391:    if (mipWidth > 1)
./Textures.cxx:392:      mipWidth /= 2;
./Textures.cxx:393:    if (mipHeight > 1)
./Textures.cxx:394:      mipHeight /= 2;
./Textures.cxx:395:  }
./Textures.cxx:396:
./Textures.cxx:397:  barrier.subresourceRange.baseMipLevel = mipLevels - 1;
./Textures.cxx:398:  barrier.oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL;
./Textures.cxx:399:  barrier.newLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL;
./Textures.cxx:400:  barrier.srcAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT;
./Textures.cxx:401:  barrier.dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_SHADER_READ_BIT;
./Textures.cxx:402:
./Textures.cxx:403:  vkCmdPipelineBarrier(commandBuffer, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT,
./Textures.cxx:404:                       VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_FRAGMENT_SHADER_BIT, 0, 0, nullptr, 0,
./Textures.cxx:405:                       nullptr, 1, &barrier);
./Textures.cxx:406:
./Textures.cxx:407:  endSingleTimeCommands(commandBuffer);
./Textures.cxx:408:}

./intiVk3.cxx:12:      querySwapChainSupport(physicalDevice);
./intiVk3.cxx:13:  // pick the best options
./intiVk3.cxx:14:  VkSurfaceFormatKHR surfaceFormat =
./intiVk3.cxx:15:      chooseSwapSurfaceFormat(swapChainSupport.formats);
./intiVk3.cxx:16:  VkPresentModeKHR presentMode =
./intiVk3.cxx:17:      chooseSwapPresentMode(swapChainSupport.presentModes);
./intiVk3.cxx:18:  VkExtent2D extent = chooseSwapExtent(swapChainSupport.capabilities);
./intiVk3.cxx:19:

./intiVk3.cxx:23:
./intiVk3.cxx:24:  // add one to allow triple buffering
./intiVk3.cxx:25:  // Metal limits the max to 3 so only +1 one is allowed while NVIDIA and AMD
./intiVk3.cxx:26:  // has no limits
./intiVk3.cxx:27:  uint32_t imageCount = swapChainSupport.capabilities.minImageCount + 1;
./intiVk3.cxx:28:
./intiVk3.cxx:29:  // Check to not exeed the maximum
./intiVk3.cxx:30:  // 0 is a special number meaning that there is not upper limit
./intiVk3.cxx:31:  if (swapChainSupport.capabilities.maxImageCount > 0 &&
./intiVk3.cxx:32:      imageCount > swapChainSupport.capabilities.maxImageCount) {
./intiVk3.cxx:33:    imageCount = swapChainSupport.capabilities.maxImageCount;
./intiVk3.cxx:34:  }
./intiVk3.cxx:35:
./intiVk3.cxx:36:  VkSwapchainCreateInfoKHR createInfo{};
./intiVk3.cxx:37:  createInfo.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_SWAPCHAIN_CREATE_INFO_KHR;

./intiVk3.cxx:39:

./intiVk3.cxx:41:  createInfo.minImageCount = imageCount;

./intiVk3.cxx:44:  createInfo.imageExtent = extent;
./intiVk3.cxx:45:  createInfo.imageArrayLayers = 1;
./intiVk3.cxx:46:  createInfo.imageUsage =
./intiVk3.cxx:47:      VK_IMAGE_USAGE_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_BIT; // for post VFX use
./intiVk3.cxx:48:                                           // VK_IMAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT
./intiVk3.cxx:49:
./intiVk3.cxx:50:  // ---- graphics and present queue familiy equality check
./intiVk3.cxx:51:  QueueFamilyIndices indices = findQueueFamilies(physicalDevice);
./intiVk3.cxx:52:  uint32_t queueFamilyIndices[] = {indices.graphicsFamily.value(),
./intiVk3.cxx:53:                                   indices.presentFamily.value()};
./intiVk3.cxx:54:
./intiVk3.cxx:55:  // If not same then use concurrent which means that we dont have to worry
./intiVk3.cxx:56:  // about the manual ownership If same then use exclusive which is faster
./intiVk3.cxx:57:  if (indices.graphicsFamily != indices.presentFamily) {
./intiVk3.cxx:58:    createInfo.imageSharingMode = VK_SHARING_MODE_CONCURRENT;
./intiVk3.cxx:59:    createInfo.queueFamilyIndexCount = 2;
./intiVk3.cxx:60:    createInfo.pQueueFamilyIndices = queueFamilyIndices;
./intiVk3.cxx:61:  } else {
./intiVk3.cxx:62:    createInfo.imageSharingMode = VK_SHARING_MODE_EXCLUSIVE;
./intiVk3.cxx:63:    createInfo.queueFamilyIndexCount = 0;     // Optional
./intiVk3.cxx:64:    createInfo.pQueueFamilyIndices = nullptr; // Optional
./intiVk3.cxx:65:  }
./intiVk3.cxx:66:
./intiVk3.cxx:67:  // how to transform the present
./intiVk3.cxx:68:  // IE: tell vulkan how to handle rotations. We just use what the windowing
./intiVk3.cxx:69:  // system provides
./intiVk3.cxx:70:  createInfo.preTransform = swapChainSupport.capabilities.currentTransform;
./intiVk3.cxx:71:  createInfo.compositeAlpha =

./intiVk3.cxx:73:                                         // other windows
./intiVk3.cxx:74:

./intiVk3.cxx:78:

./intiVk3.cxx:80:

./intiVk3.cxx:93:// Images views: VkImage is just raw memory. Image views provide the GPU with
./intiVk3.cxx:94:// all the neseccary context and information to present the data correctly We
./intiVk3.cxx:95:// did specify in the swapchain what formats and options to use, then why an
./intiVk3.cxx:96:// image view? Because a VkImage by itself proved no indication of the location
./intiVk3.cxx:97:// and size of the contents An Image view providies those important information
./intiVk3.cxx:98:// to the GPU

./shaders/simple_shader.frag:9:void main() {
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:10:    vec4 texColor = texture(texSampler, vec3(fragTexCoord, float(blockID)));
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:11:    if (texColor.a < 0.1) discard;
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:12:
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:13:    vec3 color = texColor.rgb * brightnessO;
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:14:
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:15:    // Fake bloom — luminance above threshold bleeds into extra brightness
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:16:    float luma      = dot(color, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:17:    float threshold = 0.3;
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:18:    float bloom     = max(luma - threshold, 0.0) * 2.5;
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:19:    color += bloom * color;   // tinted glow, not white blowout
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:20:
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:21:    // Depth fog
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:22:    float depth     = gl_FragCoord.z / gl_FragCoord.w;
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:23:    float fogFactor = clamp((192.0 - depth) / (192.0 - 48.0), 0.0, 1.0);
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:24:    vec3  fogColor  = vec3(0.78, 0.88, 1.0);
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:25:    color = mix(fogColor, color, fogFactor);
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:26:
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:27:    outColor = vec4(color, texColor.a);
./shaders/simple_shader.frag:28:}

./Camera.cxx:42:void Camera::updateUBO(UniformBufferObject &UBO, VkExtent2D &swapChainExtent,
./Camera.cxx:43:                       Event &event) {

./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:245:VkVertexInputBindingDescription Vertex::getBindingDescription() {
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:246:  VkVertexInputBindingDescription bindingDescription{};
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:247:  // A vertex binding describes at which rate to load data from memory
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:248:  // throughout the verticies. It specifies the number of bytes between data
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:249:  // entries and whether to move to the next data entery after each vertex or
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:250:  // after each instance.
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:251:
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:252:  bindingDescription.binding = 0; // binding index to zero
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:253:  bindingDescription.stride = sizeof(Vertex);
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:254:  bindingDescription.inputRate =
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:255:      VK_VERTEX_INPUT_RATE_VERTEX; // advance to the next input per vertex
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:256:
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:257:  return bindingDescription;
./GraphicsPipeline.cxx:258:}

./UIComponents.cxx:75:  float w = size.x, h = size.y;

./UIComponents.cxx:82:      {{cx + w, cy + h, z}, {1.0f, 1.0f}, (uint32_t)textureIDX}, // top-right

./UIComponents.cxx:84:  };

./UIComponents.cxx:94:void UIComponents::draw(DrawInfo &drawInfo, PushConstantC2 &c2) {
./UIComponents.cxx:95:  VkBuffer vertexBuffers[] = {vertexBuffer};
./UIComponents.cxx:96:  VkDeviceSize offsets[] = {0};
./UIComponents.cxx:97:
./UIComponents.cxx:98:  if (vertices.size() == 0 || indices.size() == 0) [[unlikely]]
./UIComponents.cxx:99:    return;
./UIComponents.cxx:100:
./UIComponents.cxx:101:  vkCmdBindVertexBuffers(drawInfo.commandBuffer, 0, 1, vertexBuffers, offsets);
./UIComponents.cxx:102:  vkCmdBindIndexBuffer(drawInfo.commandBuffer, indexBuffer, 0,
./UIComponents.cxx:103:                       VK_INDEX_TYPE_UINT32);
./UIComponents.cxx:104:
./UIComponents.cxx:105:  vkCmdPushConstants(drawInfo.commandBuffer, drawInfo.pipelineLayout,
./UIComponents.cxx:106:                     VK_SHADER_STAGE_VERTEX_BIT, 0, sizeof(PushConstantC2),
./UIComponents.cxx:107:                     &c2);
./UIComponents.cxx:108:
./UIComponents.cxx:109:  vkCmdBindDescriptorSets(

./HelloTriangleApplication.cxx:33:

./Fonts.h:16:  uint32_t bitmapW = 0, bitmapH = 0;
./Fonts.h:17:

./Fonts.cxx:14:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
./Fonts.cxx:15:// createTextureImage
./Fonts.cxx:16://
./Fonts.cxx:17://  1. Load all printable ASCII glyphs.
./Fonts.cxx:18://  2. Pack them side-by-side in a single-row atlas (power-of-two width).
./Fonts.cxx:19://  3. Upload the atlas as a VK_FORMAT_R8_UNORM texture.
./Fonts.cxx:20:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
./Fonts.cxx:21:void Fonts::createTextureImage() {
./Fonts.cxx:22:
./Fonts.cxx:23:  std::string fontPath = "/home/divyansh/MinecraftClone/fonts/arial.ttf";
./Fonts.cxx:24:  // ── 1. Load face
./Fonts.cxx:25:  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
./Fonts.cxx:26:  FT_Face face;
./Fonts.cxx:27:  if (FT_New_Face(ft, fontPath.c_str(), 0, &face)) {
./Fonts.cxx:28:    throw std::runtime_error("FreeType: failed to load font: " + fontPath);
./Fonts.cxx:29:  }
./Fonts.cxx:30:  FT_Set_Pixel_Sizes(face, 0, FONT_PIXEL_HEIGHT);
./Fonts.cxx:31:
./Fonts.cxx:32:  // ── 2. First pass: measure atlas dimensions ───────────────────────────────
./Fonts.cxx:33:  uint32_t totalWidth = 0;
./Fonts.cxx:34:  uint32_t maxHeight = 0;
./Fonts.cxx:35:
./Fonts.cxx:36:  for (uint32_t c = FIRST_CHAR; c <= LAST_CHAR; ++c) {
./Fonts.cxx:37:    if (FT_Load_Char(face, c, FT_LOAD_RENDER)) {
./Fonts.cxx:38:      std::cerr << "FreeType: failed to load glyph '" << (char)c << "'\n";
./Fonts.cxx:39:      continue;
./Fonts.cxx:40:    }
./Fonts.cxx:41:    FT_GlyphSlot g = face->glyph;
./Fonts.cxx:42:    totalWidth += g->bitmap.width + 1; // +1 px gap between glyphs
./Fonts.cxx:43:    maxHeight = std::max(maxHeight, g->bitmap.rows);
./Fonts.cxx:44:  }
./Fonts.cxx:45:
./Fonts.cxx:46:  // Round atlas width up to the next power-of-two (better for mipmaps)
./Fonts.cxx:47:  atlasW = 1;
./Fonts.cxx:48:  while (atlasW < totalWidth)
./Fonts.cxx:49:    atlasW <<= 1;
./Fonts.cxx:50:  atlasH = 1;
./Fonts.cxx:51:  while (atlasH < maxHeight)
./Fonts.cxx:52:    atlasH <<= 1;
./Fonts.cxx:53:
./Fonts.cxx:54:  // ── 3. Allocate CPU-side atlas (R8, zero-initialised) ────────────────────
./Fonts.cxx:55:  std::vector<uint8_t> atlasPixels(atlasW * atlasH, 0);
./Fonts.cxx:56:
./Fonts.cxx:57:  // ── 4. Second pass: blit each glyph + record metrics ─────────────────────
./Fonts.cxx:58:  uint32_t penX = 0;
./Fonts.cxx:59:
./Fonts.cxx:60:  for (uint32_t c = FIRST_CHAR; c <= LAST_CHAR; ++c) {
./Fonts.cxx:61:    if (FT_Load_Char(face, c, FT_LOAD_RENDER))
./Fonts.cxx:62:      continue;
./Fonts.cxx:63:
./Fonts.cxx:64:    FT_GlyphSlot g = face->glyph;
./Fonts.cxx:65:    uint32_t bw = g->bitmap.width;
./Fonts.cxx:66:    uint32_t bh = g->bitmap.rows;
./Fonts.cxx:67:    int pitch = g->bitmap.pitch; // may differ from bw
./Fonts.cxx:68:
./Fonts.cxx:69:    // Blit the glyph bitmap into the atlas row-by-row
./Fonts.cxx:70:    for (uint32_t row = 0; row < bh; ++row) {
./Fonts.cxx:71:      uint8_t *src = g->bitmap.buffer + row * pitch;
./Fonts.cxx:72:      uint8_t *dest = atlasPixels.data() + row * atlasW + penX;
./Fonts.cxx:73:      memcpy(dest, src, bw);
./Fonts.cxx:74:    }
./Fonts.cxx:75:
./Fonts.cxx:76:    // Store glyph info
./Fonts.cxx:77:    GlyphInfo &info = glyphs[c - FIRST_CHAR];
./Fonts.cxx:78:    info.bitmapW = bw;
./Fonts.cxx:79:    info.bitmapH = bh;
./Fonts.cxx:80:    info.bearingX = g->bitmap_left;
./Fonts.cxx:81:    info.bearingY = g->bitmap_top;
./Fonts.cxx:82:    info.advanceX = static_cast<int32_t>(g->advance.x); // 1/64 px
./Fonts.cxx:83:
./Fonts.cxx:84:    // UV in [0..1] into the atlas
./Fonts.cxx:85:    info.u0 = static_cast<float>(penX) / static_cast<float>(atlasW);
./Fonts.cxx:86:    info.v0 = 0.0f;
./Fonts.cxx:87:    info.u1 = static_cast<float>(penX + bw) / static_cast<float>(atlasW);
./Fonts.cxx:88:    info.v1 = static_cast<float>(bh) / static_cast<float>(atlasH);
./Fonts.cxx:89:
./Fonts.cxx:90:    penX += bw + 1;
./Fonts.cxx:91:  }
./Fonts.cxx:92:
./Fonts.cxx:93:  FT_Done_Face(face);
./Fonts.cxx:94:
./Fonts.cxx:95:  // ── 5. Upload atlas to GPU ────────────────────────────────────────────────
./Fonts.cxx:96:  VkDeviceSize imageSize = static_cast<VkDeviceSize>(atlasW) * atlasH;
./Fonts.cxx:97:
./Fonts.cxx:98:  texture.mipLevels =
./Fonts.cxx:99:      static_cast<uint32_t>(std::floor(std::log2(std::max(atlasW, atlasH)))) +
./Fonts.cxx:100:      1;
./Fonts.cxx:101:
./Fonts.cxx:102:  // Staging buffer
./Fonts.cxx:103:  VkBuffer stagingBuffer;
./Fonts.cxx:104:  VkDeviceMemory stagingMemory;
./Fonts.cxx:105:  texture.createBuffer(imageSize, VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT,
./Fonts.cxx:106:                       VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_VISIBLE_BIT |
./Fonts.cxx:107:                           VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_COHERENT_BIT,
./Fonts.cxx:108:                       stagingBuffer, stagingMemory);
./Fonts.cxx:109:
./Fonts.cxx:110:  void *mapped;
./Fonts.cxx:111:  vkMapMemory(texture.device, stagingMemory, 0, imageSize, 0, &mapped);
./Fonts.cxx:112:  memcpy(mapped, atlasPixels.data(), static_cast<size_t>(imageSize));
./Fonts.cxx:113:  vkUnmapMemory(texture.device, stagingMemory);
./Fonts.cxx:114:
./Fonts.cxx:115:  // Device image
./Fonts.cxx:116:  texture.createImage(atlasW, atlasH, VK_SAMPLE_COUNT_1_BIT, texture.mipLevels,
./Fonts.cxx:117:                      VK_FORMAT_R8_UNORM, VK_IMAGE_TILING_OPTIMAL,
./Fonts.cxx:118:                      VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT |
./Fonts.cxx:119:                          VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT |
./Fonts.cxx:120:                          VK_IMAGE_USAGE_SAMPLED_BIT,
./Fonts.cxx:121:                      VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_DEVICE_LOCAL_BIT, texture.textureImage,
./Fonts.cxx:122:                      texture.textureImageMemory);
./Fonts.cxx:123:
./Fonts.cxx:124:  texture.transitionImageLayout(
./Fonts.cxx:125:      texture.textureImage, VK_FORMAT_R8_UNORM, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED,
./Fonts.cxx:126:      VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL, texture.mipLevels);
./Fonts.cxx:127:
./Fonts.cxx:128:  texture.copyBufferToImage(stagingBuffer, texture.textureImage, atlasW,
./Fonts.cxx:129:                            atlasH);
./Fonts.cxx:130:
./Fonts.cxx:131:  texture.generateMipmaps(texture.textureImage, VK_FORMAT_R8_UNORM, atlasW,
./Fonts.cxx:132:                          atlasH, texture.mipLevels);
./Fonts.cxx:133:
./Fonts.cxx:134:  vkDestroyBuffer(texture.device, stagingBuffer, nullptr);
./Fonts.cxx:135:  vkFreeMemory(texture.device, stagingMemory, nullptr);
./Fonts.cxx:136:}
./Fonts.cxx:137:
./Fonts.cxx:138:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
./Fonts.cxx:139:// buildTextMesh
./Fonts.cxx:140://
./Fonts.cxx:141://  Converts a UTF-8 string (ASCII subset) into a series of quads.
./Fonts.cxx:142://  Each character gets one quad whose size and position are derived from
./Fonts.cxx:143://  the stored GlyphInfo (bearingX/Y, advanceX).
./Fonts.cxx:144://
./Fonts.cxx:145://  screenScale is the world-space height of FONT_PIXEL_HEIGHT pixels.
./Fonts.cxx:146://  The pen starts at the world origin (0, 0, 0).
./Fonts.cxx:147:// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
./Fonts.cxx:148:float Fonts::buildTextMesh(const std::string &text, float screenScale) {
./Fonts.cxx:149:
./Fonts.cxx:150:  // pixels → world-space conversion factor
./Fonts.cxx:151:  const float px2ws = screenScale / static_cast<float>(FONT_PIXEL_HEIGHT);
./Fonts.cxx:152:
./Fonts.cxx:153:  std::vector<FontVertex> vertices;
./Fonts.cxx:154:  std::vector<uint32_t> indices;
./Fonts.cxx:155:  vertices.reserve(text.size() * 4);
./Fonts.cxx:156:  indices.reserve(text.size() * 6);
./Fonts.cxx:157:
./Fonts.cxx:158:  float penX = 0.0f; // world-space pen position (advances left → right)
./Fonts.cxx:159:
./Fonts.cxx:160:  for (char ch : text) {
./Fonts.cxx:161:    uint32_t c = static_cast<unsigned char>(ch);
./Fonts.cxx:162:
./Fonts.cxx:163:    // Skip unprintable / out-of-range characters
./Fonts.cxx:164:    if (c < FIRST_CHAR || c > LAST_CHAR) {
./Fonts.cxx:165:      // Still advance by half an EM for spaces / control chars
./Fonts.cxx:166:      if (c == ' ')
./Fonts.cxx:167:        penX += screenScale * 0.3f;
./Fonts.cxx:168:      continue;
./Fonts.cxx:169:    }
./Fonts.cxx:170:
./Fonts.cxx:171:    const GlyphInfo &g = glyphs[c - FIRST_CHAR];
./Fonts.cxx:172:
./Fonts.cxx:173:    // Skip glyphs with no bitmap (e.g. space was loaded but has 0 size)
./Fonts.cxx:174:    if (g.bitmapW == 0 || g.bitmapH == 0) {
./Fonts.cxx:175:      penX += static_cast<float>(g.advanceX) / 64.0f * px2ws;
./Fonts.cxx:176:      continue;
./Fonts.cxx:177:    }
./Fonts.cxx:178:
./Fonts.cxx:179:    // World-space quad corners
./Fonts.cxx:180:    //   bearingY  = pixels from baseline to top of bitmap
./Fonts.cxx:181:    //   bearingX  = pixels from pen to left edge of bitmap
./Fonts.cxx:182:    float x0 = penX + static_cast<float>(g.bearingX) * px2ws;
./Fonts.cxx:183:    float x1 = x0 + static_cast<float>(g.bitmapW) * px2ws;
./Fonts.cxx:184:
./Fonts.cxx:185:    // In Vulkan NDC  Y grows downward, so top = +bearingY, bottom =
./Fonts.cxx:186:    // -(bitmapH-bearingY)
./Fonts.cxx:187:    float y1 = static_cast<float>(g.bearingY) * px2ws;     // top
./Fonts.cxx:188:    float y0 = y1 - static_cast<float>(g.bitmapH) * px2ws; // bottom
./Fonts.cxx:189:
./Fonts.cxx:190:    uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(vertices.size());
./Fonts.cxx:191:
./Fonts.cxx:192:    //  bottom-left, bottom-right, top-right, top-left
./Fonts.cxx:193:    vertices.push_back(FontVertex{{x0, y0, 0.f}, {g.u0, g.v1}});
./Fonts.cxx:194:    vertices.push_back(FontVertex{{x1, y0, 0.f}, {g.u1, g.v1}});
./Fonts.cxx:195:    vertices.push_back(FontVertex{{x1, y1, 0.f}, {g.u1, g.v0}});
./Fonts.cxx:196:    vertices.push_back(FontVertex{{x0, y1, 0.f}, {g.u0, g.v0}});
./Fonts.cxx:197:
./Fonts.cxx:198:    // Two triangles: (0,1,2) and (2,3,0)
./Fonts.cxx:199:    indices.insert(indices.end(), {base + 0, base + 1, base + 2, base + 2,
./Fonts.cxx:200:                                   base + 3, base + 0});
./Fonts.cxx:201:
./Fonts.cxx:202:    // Advance pen by the glyph's advance width (stored in 1/64 px)
./Fonts.cxx:203:    penX += static_cast<float>(g.advanceX) / 64.0f * px2ws;
./Fonts.cxx:204:  }
./Fonts.cxx:205:
./Fonts.cxx:206:  // Upload to GPU
./Fonts.cxx:207:  mesh.loadVertices(vertices);
./Fonts.cxx:208:  mesh.loadIndices(indices);
./Fonts.cxx:209:  mesh.createVertexBuffer();
./Fonts.cxx:210:  mesh.createIndexBuffer();
./Fonts.cxx:211:
./Fonts.cxx:212:  return penX; // total string width in world space
./Fonts.cxx:213:}

./intiVk.cxx:427:
./intiVk.cxx:428:int HelloTriangleApplication::rateDeviceSuitablity(VkPhysicalDevice device) {
./intiVk.cxx:429:  VkPhysicalDeviceProperties props;
./intiVk.cxx:430:  VkPhysicalDeviceFeatures feats;
./intiVk.cxx:431:  vkGetPhysicalDeviceProperties(device, &props);
./intiVk.cxx:432:  vkGetPhysicalDeviceFeatures(device, &feats);
./intiVk.cxx:433:
./intiVk.cxx:434:  int score = 0;
./intiVk.cxx:435:
./intiVk.cxx:436:  std::cout << "Device: " << props.deviceName << std::endl;
./intiVk.cxx:437:
./intiVk.cxx:438:  // Prefer discrete or integrated GPU
./intiVk.cxx:439:  if (props.deviceType == VK_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_TYPE_DISCRETE_GPU)
./intiVk.cxx:440:    score += 20000;
./intiVk.cxx:441:  else if (props.deviceType == VK_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_TYPE_INTEGRATED_GPU)
./intiVk.cxx:442:    score += 5000;
./intiVk.cxx:443:
./intiVk.cxx:444:  // Bigger textures = better
./intiVk.cxx:445:  score += props.limits.maxImageDimension2D;
./intiVk.cxx:446:
./intiVk.cxx:447:  // Useful features
./intiVk.cxx:448:  if (feats.samplerAnisotropy)
./intiVk.cxx:449:    score += 1000;
./intiVk.cxx:450:  if (feats.shaderInt64)
./intiVk.cxx:451:    score += 500;
./intiVk.cxx:452:  if (feats.wideLines)
./intiVk.cxx:453:    score += 300;
./intiVk.cxx:454:
./intiVk.cxx:455:#ifdef __APPLE__
./intiVk.cxx:456:  // Metal backend doesn’t support geometry/tessellation shaders
./intiVk.cxx:457:  score += 1500;
./intiVk.cxx:458:#else
./intiVk.cxx:459:  if (!feats.geometryShader)
./intiVk.cxx:460:    return 0; // skip if no geometry shader
./intiVk.cxx:461:#endif
./intiVk.cxx:462:
./intiVk.cxx:463:  return score;
./intiVk.cxx:464:}

./intiVk.cxx:466:// Queue -> a line in which a set of commands needs to be executed
./intiVk.cxx:467:// there maybe multiple types of queues(Graphics, Computem, ...)
./intiVk.cxx:468:// Queue Family -> Conatains multiple queues for the GPU(Note: A queue family

./intiVk.cxx:471:// finding queue families
./intiVk.cxx:472:HelloTriangleApplication::QueueFamilyIndices
./intiVk.cxx:473:HelloTriangleApplication::findQueueFamilies(VkPhysicalDevice device) {
./intiVk.cxx:474:  QueueFamilyIndices indices;
./intiVk.cxx:475:  uint32_t queueFamilyCount = 0;
./intiVk.cxx:476:  vkGetPhysicalDeviceQueueFamilyProperties(
./intiVk.cxx:477:      device, &queueFamilyCount,
./intiVk.cxx:478:      nullptr); // grts all the queue families supported by the GPU
./intiVk.cxx:479:
./intiVk.cxx:480:  std::vector<VkQueueFamilyProperties> queueFamilies(queueFamilyCount);
./intiVk.cxx:481:  vkGetPhysicalDeviceQueueFamilyProperties(device, &queueFamilyCount,
./intiVk.cxx:482:                                           queueFamilies.data());
./intiVk.cxx:483:
./intiVk.cxx:484:  int i = 0;
./intiVk.cxx:485:  for (const auto &queueFamily : queueFamilies) {
./intiVk.cxx:486:    if (queueFamily.queueFlags & VK_QUEUE_GRAPHICS_BIT)
./intiVk.cxx:487:      indices.graphicsFamily = i;
./intiVk.cxx:488:
./intiVk.cxx:489:    VkBool32 presentSupport = false;
./intiVk.cxx:490:    vkGetPhysicalDeviceSurfaceSupportKHR(device, i, surface, &presentSupport);
./intiVk.cxx:491:    if (presentSupport)
./intiVk.cxx:492:      indices.presentFamily = i;
./intiVk.cxx:493:
./intiVk.cxx:494:    if (indices.isComplete())
./intiVk.cxx:495:      break;
./intiVk.cxx:496:
./intiVk.cxx:497:    i++;
./intiVk.cxx:498:  }
./intiVk.cxx:499:  // NOTE: some GPU supports different graphics and presentation queues
./intiVk.cxx:500:
./intiVk.cxx:501:  return indices;
./intiVk.cxx:502:}

./intiVk.cxx:559:VKAPI_ATTR VkBool32 VKAPI_CALL HelloTriangleApplication::debugCallback(
./intiVk.cxx:560:    VkDebugUtilsMessageSeverityFlagBitsEXT messageSeverity,
./intiVk.cxx:561:    VkDebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT messageType,
./intiVk.cxx:562:    const VkDebugUtilsMessengerCallbackDataEXT *pCallbackData,
./intiVk.cxx:563:    void * /*pUserData*/) {
./intiVk.cxx:564:  const char *severity = "VERBOSE";
./intiVk.cxx:565:  if (messageSeverity & VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_ERROR_BIT_EXT)
./intiVk.cxx:566:    severity = "ERROR  ";
./intiVk.cxx:567:  else if (messageSeverity & VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_WARNING_BIT_EXT)
./intiVk.cxx:568:    severity = "WARNING";
./intiVk.cxx:569:  else if (messageSeverity & VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_INFO_BIT_EXT)
./intiVk.cxx:570:    severity = "INFO   ";
./intiVk.cxx:571:
./intiVk.cxx:572:  const char *type = "GENERAL   ";
./intiVk.cxx:573:  if (messageType & VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_TYPE_VALIDATION_BIT_EXT)
./intiVk.cxx:574:    type = "VALIDATION";
./intiVk.cxx:575:  else if (messageType & VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_TYPE_PERFORMANCE_BIT_EXT)
./intiVk.cxx:576:    type = "PERF      ";
./intiVk.cxx:577:
./intiVk.cxx:578:  std::cerr << "\n[VK " << severity << "][" << type << "] "
./intiVk.cxx:579:            << pCallbackData->pMessage << "\n";
./intiVk.cxx:580:
./intiVk.cxx:581:  // Print stack trace for warnings and errors only
./intiVk.cxx:582:  if (messageSeverity >= VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_WARNING_BIT_EXT)
./intiVk.cxx:583:    printStackTrace();
./intiVk.cxx:584:
./intiVk.cxx:585:  return VK_FALSE;
./intiVk.cxx:586:}

./intiVk.cxx:614:void HelloTriangleApplication::createLogicalDevice() {
./intiVk.cxx:615:  std::cout << "Creating Logical Device..." << std::endl;
./intiVk.cxx:616:  // Queue Family Stuff
./intiVk.cxx:617:  QueueFamilyIndices indices =
./intiVk.cxx:618:      findQueueFamilies(physicalDevice); // get the required queue families
./intiVk.cxx:619:  std::vector<VkDeviceQueueCreateInfo> queueCreateInfos;
./intiVk.cxx:620:  std::set<uint32_t> uniqueQueueFamilies = {indices.graphicsFamily.value(),
./intiVk.cxx:621:                                            indices.presentFamily.value()};
./intiVk.cxx:622:
./intiVk.cxx:623:  // right now only creating a single queue(can create muliple)
./intiVk.cxx:624:  float queuePriority = 1.f;
./intiVk.cxx:625:  for (uint32_t queueFamily : uniqueQueueFamilies) // prepare each queue family
./intiVk.cxx:626:  {
./intiVk.cxx:627:    VkDeviceQueueCreateInfo queueCreateInfo{};
./intiVk.cxx:628:    queueCreateInfo.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DEVICE_QUEUE_CREATE_INFO;
./intiVk.cxx:629:    queueCreateInfo.queueFamilyIndex = queueFamily;
./intiVk.cxx:630:    queueCreateInfo.queueCount = 1;
./intiVk.cxx:631:    queueCreateInfo.pQueuePriorities = &queuePriority;
./intiVk.cxx:632:
./intiVk.cxx:633:    queueCreateInfos.push_back(
./intiVk.cxx:634:        queueCreateInfo); // push the queue info in the vector
./intiVk.cxx:635:  }
./intiVk.cxx:636:
./intiVk.cxx:637:  // ---- Specifing which feature we wanna use ----
./intiVk.cxx:638:  VkPhysicalDeviceFeatures deviceFeatures{};
./intiVk.cxx:639:  deviceFeatures.samplerAnisotropy = VK_TRUE;
./intiVk.cxx:640:  deviceFeatures.sampleRateShading = VK_TRUE;
./intiVk.cxx:641:

./intiVk.cxx:747:// Swapchain -> A queue of images presented to the screen in a sequence

./intiVk.cxx:751:// GPU has

./intiVk.cxx:755:
./intiVk.cxx:756:  // ---- Get the capabilities ----
./intiVk.cxx:757:  vkGetPhysicalDeviceSurfaceCapabilitiesKHR(device, surface,
./intiVk.cxx:758:                                            &details.capabilities);
./intiVk.cxx:759:
./intiVk.cxx:760:  // ---- get the format ----
./intiVk.cxx:761:  uint32_t formatCount;
./intiVk.cxx:762:  vkGetPhysicalDeviceSurfaceFormatsKHR(device, surface, &formatCount, nullptr);
./intiVk.cxx:763:
./intiVk.cxx:764:  if (formatCount != 0) {
./intiVk.cxx:765:    details.formats.resize(formatCount);
./intiVk.cxx:766:    vkGetPhysicalDeviceSurfaceFormatsKHR(device, surface, &formatCount,
./intiVk.cxx:767:                                         details.formats.data());
./intiVk.cxx:768:  }
./intiVk.cxx:769:

./intiVk.cxx:776:    vkGetPhysicalDeviceSurfacePresentModesKHR(
./intiVk.cxx:777:        device, surface, &presentModeCount, details.presentModes.data());
./intiVk.cxx:778:  }
./intiVk.cxx:779:
./intiVk.cxx:780:  return details;
./intiVk.cxx:781:}

./intiVk.cxx:813:// Extent defines the resolution of the image
./intiVk.cxx:814:// MacOS has a fixed extent while other windowing systems have a varible extent
./intiVk.cxx:815:// which is clamped to the window bounds also High-DPI displays like Apple
./intiVk.cxx:816:// Liquid Retina displays have to be configure in a sepreate way
./intiVk.cxx:817:VkExtent2D HelloTriangleApplication::chooseSwapExtent(
./intiVk.cxx:818:    const VkSurfaceCapabilitiesKHR &capabilities) {
./intiVk.cxx:819:#ifdef __APPLE__
./intiVk.cxx:820:  // MoltenVK on macOS (Retina-safe)
./intiVk.cxx:821:  int width, height;
./intiVk.cxx:822:  glfwGetFramebufferSize(_window, &width, &height);
./intiVk.cxx:823:
./intiVk.cxx:824:  VkExtent2D actualExtent = {static_cast<uint32_t>(width),
./intiVk.cxx:825:                             static_cast<uint32_t>(height)};
./intiVk.cxx:826:
./intiVk.cxx:827:  return actualExtent; // Always use framebuffer size on Retina
./intiVk.cxx:828:#else
./intiVk.cxx:829:  // Metal IE: the size is predifined and is not UINT 32 max. therefore, use the
./intiVk.cxx:830:  // size provided by the system
./intiVk.cxx:831:  if (capabilities.currentExtent.width !=
./intiVk.cxx:832:      std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {
./intiVk.cxx:833:    return capabilities.currentExtent;
./intiVk.cxx:834:  }
./intiVk.cxx:835:  // The surface does not have a fixed size and you must create it by yourself
./intiVk.cxx:836:  else {
./intiVk.cxx:837:    int width, height;
./intiVk.cxx:838:    glfwGetFramebufferSize(_window, &width, &height);
./intiVk.cxx:839:
./intiVk.cxx:840:    VkExtent2D actualExtent = {static_cast<uint32_t>(width),
./intiVk.cxx:841:                               static_cast<uint32_t>(height)};
./intiVk.cxx:842:
./intiVk.cxx:843:    actualExtent.width =
./intiVk.cxx:844:        std::clamp(actualExtent.width, capabilities.minImageExtent.width,
./intiVk.cxx:845:                   capabilities.maxImageExtent.width);
./intiVk.cxx:846:
./intiVk.cxx:847:    actualExtent.height =
./intiVk.cxx:848:        std::clamp(actualExtent.height, capabilities.minImageExtent.height,
./intiVk.cxx:849:                   capabilities.maxImageExtent.height);
./intiVk.cxx:850:
./intiVk.cxx:851:    return actualExtent;
./intiVk.cxx:852:  }
./intiVk.cxx:853:
./intiVk.cxx:854:#endif
./intiVk.cxx:855:}

./intiVk.cxx:859:void HelloTriangleApplication::createVertexBuffer() {
./intiVk.cxx:860:  std::cout << "Creating Vertex Buffer..." << std::endl;
./intiVk.cxx:861:
./intiVk.cxx:862:  VkDeviceSize bufferSize = sizeof(vertices[0]) * vertices.size();
./intiVk.cxx:863:
./intiVk.cxx:864:  VkBuffer stagingBuffer;
./intiVk.cxx:865:  VkDeviceMemory stagingBufferMemory;
./intiVk.cxx:866:  // TX
./intiVk.cxx:867:  createBuffer(bufferSize, VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT,
./intiVk.cxx:868:               VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_VISIBLE_BIT |
./intiVk.cxx:869:                   VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_HOST_CACHED_BIT,
./intiVk.cxx:870:               stagingBuffer, stagingBufferMemory);
./intiVk.cxx:871:
./intiVk.cxx:872:  // filling in the vertex buffer
./intiVk.cxx:873:  void *data;
./intiVk.cxx:874:  vkMapMemory(device, stagingBufferMemory, 0, bufferSize, 0, &data);
./intiVk.cxx:875:  memcpy(data, vertices.data(), (size_t)bufferSize);
./intiVk.cxx:876:  vkUnmapMemory(device, stagingBufferMemory);
./intiVk.cxx:877:
./intiVk.cxx:878:  // creating the actul VBO and setting it up so the it is the destionation of
./intiVk.cxx:879:  // the transfer source TX
./intiVk.cxx:880:  createBuffer(
./intiVk.cxx:881:      bufferSize,
./intiVk.cxx:882:      VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT | VK_BUFFER_USAGE_VERTEX_BUFFER_BIT,
./intiVk.cxx:883:      VK_MEMORY_PROPERTY_DEVICE_LOCAL_BIT, vertexBuffer, vertexBufferMemory);
./intiVk.cxx:884:
./intiVk.cxx:885:  // but we still have to copy the memory contents manully
./intiVk.cxx:886:  copyBuffer(stagingBuffer, vertexBuffer, bufferSize);
./intiVk.cxx:887:
./intiVk.cxx:888:  vkDestroyBuffer(device, stagingBuffer, nullptr);
./intiVk.cxx:889:  vkFreeMemory(device, stagingBufferMemory, nullptr);
./intiVk.cxx:890:}

./intiVk.cxx:1164:void HelloTriangleApplication::createSyncObject() {
./intiVk.cxx:1165:  // 1️⃣ Per-frame semaphores for acquiring images
./intiVk.cxx:1166:  imageAvailableSemaphores.resize(MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT);
./intiVk.cxx:1167:
./intiVk.cxx:1168:  // 2️⃣ Per-swapchain-image semaphores for rendering completion
./intiVk.cxx:1169:  renderFinishedSemaphores.resize(swapChainImages.size());
./intiVk.cxx:1170:
./intiVk.cxx:1171:  // 3️⃣ Fences to limit frames in flight
./intiVk.cxx:1172:  inFlightFences.resize(MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT);
./intiVk.cxx:1173:
./intiVk.cxx:1174:  VkSemaphoreCreateInfo semaphoreInfo{};
./intiVk.cxx:1175:  semaphoreInfo.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_SEMAPHORE_CREATE_INFO;
./intiVk.cxx:1176:
./intiVk.cxx:1177:  VkFenceCreateInfo fenceInfo{};
./intiVk.cxx:1178:  fenceInfo.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_FENCE_CREATE_INFO;
./intiVk.cxx:1179:  fenceInfo.flags = VK_FENCE_CREATE_SIGNALED_BIT; // start signaled
./intiVk.cxx:1180:
./intiVk.cxx:1181:  // Create per-frame semaphores and fences
./intiVk.cxx:1182:  for (size_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++) {
./intiVk.cxx:1183:    if (vkCreateSemaphore(device, &semaphoreInfo, nullptr,
./intiVk.cxx:1184:                          &imageAvailableSemaphores[i]) != VK_SUCCESS ||

./intiVk.cxx:1186:            VK_SUCCESS) {
./intiVk.cxx:1187:      throw std::runtime_error("failed to create semaphore or fence!");
./intiVk.cxx:1188:    }
./intiVk.cxx:1189:  }
./intiVk.cxx:1190:
./intiVk.cxx:1191:  // Create per-swapchain-image render-finished semaphores
./intiVk.cxx:1192:  for (size_t i = 0; i < swapChainImages.size(); i++) {
./intiVk.cxx:1193:    if (vkCreateSemaphore(device, &semaphoreInfo, nullptr,
./intiVk.cxx:1194:                          &renderFinishedSemaphores[i]) != VK_SUCCESS) {
./intiVk.cxx:1195:      throw std::runtime_error("failed to create renderFinished semaphore!");
./intiVk.cxx:1196:    }
./intiVk.cxx:1197:  }
